# H03 evidence / Протокол H03

Review required; no automatic HW VERIFIED promotion.

## Reviewed run summary

**Outcome: PASS for the tested H03 scope, with a CH3 status caveat.** This run used
the library's shared debug path with the KEL103 USB serial adapter (`/dev/ttyACM0`,
115200 8N1) and Keithley UART (`/dev/ttyUSB3`, 9600 8N1). The PSU identified as
Keithley 2231A-30-3, firmware field `1.04-1.04`; the load identified as KORAD-KEL103
V3.30. All six points (CH1–CH3, CV and CC) passed measurement comparison and operator
display confirmation. The 697 actual `SYST:ERR?` replies were all `0, "No error"`.
All outputs and KEL103 INPUT were independently read OFF after every point and
physically confirmed OFF at the end.

| Channel / mode | Keithley V / A / W | KEL103 V / A / W | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CH1 CV | 3.99878 / 0.098484 / 0.394079 | 3.9983 / 0.0999 / 0.3994 | PASS |
| CH1 CC | 3.01625 / 0.249764 / 0.753149 | 3.0096 / 0.2508 / 0.7548 | PASS |
| CH2 CV | 3.99183 / 0.0984068 / 0.392962 | 3.9924 / 0.0999 / 0.3988 | PASS |
| CH2 CC | 3.01097 / 0.249572 / 0.751812 | 3.0086 / 0.2508 / 0.7545 | PASS |
| CH3 CV | 3.99576 / 0.100668 / 0.402413 | 4.0013 / 0.0999 / 0.3997 | PASS; OPER caveat |
| CH3 CC | 2.98364 / 0.249532 / 0.744307 | 2.9850 / 0.2489 / 0.7429 | PASS; OPER caveat |

For CH3, `STAT:OPER:INST:ISUM3:COND?` returned `8` in both modes, while
`STAT:QUES:INST:ISUM3:COND?` returned `1` (CV) and `2` (CC); library readback and
operator display confirmations agreed with the QUESTIONABLE status and independent
load measurements. Preserve this as an instrument status-register caveat, not as a
measurement or mode-control failure. The immediately preceding CH2 CC mismatch was
caused by the load still being wired to CH3 while CH2 was under test; the verified
CH1-start replay below passed CH2 CC with the load correctly moved.
